However, curating such pairs for dynamic hand regions is prohibitively expensive and often impractical due to frame-wise inconsistencies. In this paper, we propose Implicit Preference Alignment (IPA), a data-efficient post-training framework that eliminates the need for paired preference data. Theoretically grounded in implicit reward maximization, IPA aligns the model by maximizing the likelihood of self-generated high-quality samples while penalizing deviations from the pretrained prior. Furthermore, we introduce a Hand-Aware Local Optimization mechanism to explicitly steer the alignment process toward hand regions. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves effective preference optimization to enhance hand generation quality, while significantly lowering the barrier for constructing preference data. Codes are released at https://github.com/mdswyz/IPA

</details>


### [73] [Probabilistic Object Detection with Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07549)
*Christopher Ries,Moussa Kassem Sbeyti,Nicolas Bianco,Nadja Klein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了未缩放和缩放共形预测（CP）在多类目标检测中的应用，并提出了一种结合Bonferroni校正、概率目标检测器不确定性估计以及RAPS分类预测集的统一框架。在三个自动驾驶数据集上，缩放CP相比未缩放CP在保持覆盖率的条件下，IoU提升高达19%，区间分数降低39%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典CP理论针对单输出设计，而目标检测涉及结构化多输出预测，且标准未缩放CP对低不确定性预测产生不必要的宽区间。现有工作缺乏对未缩放与缩放CP在多类目标检测中的系统比较，也未将CP与其他不确定性量化方法在该场景下集成。

Method: (i) 对边界框角点逐坐标应用CP，并用Bonferroni校正实现框级保障；(ii) 利用概率目标检测器（损失衰减训练）的逐预测偶然不确定性估计对区间进行缩放，评估未校准及两种校准变体；(iii) 扩展为两阶段流程：先用RAPS构建类别预测集，再将共形化边界框条件化于预测类别集上。

Result: 在KITTI、BDD、CODA三个自动驾驶数据集（含跨域分布漂移设置）上，缩放CP相比未缩放CP一致提升区间锐度，IoU提升最高19%，区间分数降低最高39%，且不牺牲覆盖率。类别级校准进一步提升两种变体的覆盖率，对锐度影响可忽略。

Conclusion: 缩放CP与类别级校准相结合，为实时、真实场景的目标检测提供了更具可操作性的不确定性估计，填补了CP在该领域系统比较与集成研究的空白。

Abstract: Conformal Prediction (CP) is a distribution-free method for constructing prediction sets with marginal finite-sample coverage guarantees, making it a suitable framework for reliable uncertainty quantification in safety-critical object detection. However, object detection introduces structured multi-output predictions, complicating the application of classical CP theory developed for single outputs. In addition, standard, unscaled CP produces fixed-width prediction intervals across inputs, leading to unnecessary width for low-uncertainty predictions. While scaled CP addresses this by adapting the interval width to an input-dependent uncertainty estimate, prior work has neither systematically compared unscaled and scaled CP for multi-class object detection, nor integrated CP with a complementary uncertainty quantification method in this setting. We fill this gap by: (i) applying CP coordinate-wise to bounding box corners with a Bonferroni correction for box-level guarantees; (ii) scaling the resulting intervals using per-prediction aleatoric uncertainty estimates derived from a probabilistic object detector trained with loss attenuation, evaluated in uncalibrated and two calibrated variants; (iii) extending to a two-step pipeline that constructs prediction sets for the class using RAPS and conditions the conformalized bounding boxes on the predicted class set. Across three autonomous driving datasets (KITTI, BDD, CODA), including a cross-domain setting under distribution shift, scaled CP consistently improves interval sharpness over unscaled CP, achieving up to 19% higher IoU and 39% lower interval scores, without sacrificing coverage. Class-wise calibration further improves coverage for both variants with a negligible effect on sharpness. Together, these improvements yield more actionable uncertainty estimates for real-time, real-world object detection.

</details>


### [74] [Mind the Gap: Geometrically Accurate Generative Reconstruction from Disjoint Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07550)
*Grzegorz Wilczynski,Mikołaj Zielinski,Bartosz Świrta,Dominik Belter,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新范式——从非重叠视角进行生成式三维重建（Generative Reconstruction from Disjoint Views），并构建了完整的数据集和评估指标。针对现有方法在零重叠场景下的严重失败，提出了GLADOS框架，通过生成桥接、鲁棒粗重建和迭代上下文扩展三个阶段，实现了对非重叠视角图像的有效三维重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统三维视觉系统严重依赖视觉重叠（空间重叠和外观重叠）进行几何对齐和多视图一致性约束，但在分布式群体机器人、众包数据采集等现实场景中，获取重叠视角通常不可行。现有方法在零重叠场景下完全失效，产生不连通的几何结构或语义不连贯的重建结果。

Method: GLADOS框架包含三个核心阶段：(1) 生成桥接（Generative Bridging）：利用基础模型合成中间视角以连接不连通的输入；(2) 鲁棒粗三维重建（Robust Coarse 3D Reconstruction）：通过全局对齐建立粗糙几何骨架，吸收生成过程的局部矛盾；(3) 迭代上下文扩展与一致性优化（Iterative Context Expansion and Consistency Optimization）：填充缺失区域并统一重建结果。该框架是架构无关的模块化设计。

Result: 基准测试表明，现有最先进方法在非重叠视角任务上彻底失败，产生不连通的几何或语义不连贯的重建。GLADOS框架有效解决了该问题，且由于架构无关的特性，可无缝集成生成、重建和修复领域的未来进展。

Conclusion: 非重叠视角重建是一个重要但极具挑战的新范式，GLADOS作为通用模块化框架为此提供了可行解决方案。其架构无关设计保证了良好的扩展性和未来兼容性。

Abstract: 3D vision systems are fundamentally constrained by their reliance on visual overlap: reconstruction methods require it for geometric alignment, while generative models use it to enforce multi-view consistency. This limitation is particularly acute in real-world scenarios such as distributed swarm robotics or crowd-sourced data collection, where capturing overlapping perspectives, both in terms of spatial and appearance overlap, is often impossible. We introduce Generative Reconstruction from Disjoint Views as a new paradigm, establish a comprehensive dataset, and propose specialized evaluation metrics for zero-overlap scenarios. Our benchmarking demonstrates that existing state-of-the-art methods fail catastrophically on this task, producing disconnected geometries or semantically incoherent reconstructions. To address these limitations, we propose GLADOS, a general, modular framework that operates through three stages: (1) Generative Bridging, where foundation models synthesize intermediate perspectives to connect disjoint inputs; (2) Robust Coarse 3D Reconstruction, that establish coarse geometric scaffold via global alignment which absorbs local contradictions from generative process; and (3) Iterative Context Expansion and Consistency Optimization to fill missing regions and unify the reconstruction. As an architectureagnostic framework, GLADOS enables seamless integration of future advances in generation, reconstruction, and inpainting. The source code is available at: https://github.com/gwilczynski95/GLADOS.

</details>


### [75] [VIMCAN: Visual-Inertial 3D Human Pose Estimation with Hybrid Mamba-Cross-Attention Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07552)
*Zepeng Yang,Junxuan Bai,Hao Li,Ju Dai,Junjun Pan,Yongfeng Yin,Bin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VIMCAN是一种混合架构，结合Mamba的高效序列建模与交叉注意力机制的空间推理能力，用于多模态3D人体姿态估计，在TotalCapture和3DPW数据集上分别达到17.2mm和45.3mm的MPJPE，并支持实时推理（>60 FPS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的SOTA人体姿态估计方法在处理长序列时因二次复杂度难以实现实时处理；Mamba虽然能高效处理序列，但在多模态场景下难以捕获复杂的空间依赖关系。

Method: 提出VIMCAN混合架构，利用Mamba的动态参数化进行时序建模，同时引入交叉注意力机制提取空间依赖特征，实现RGB关键点与可穿戴IMU数据之间的鲁棒视觉-惯性融合与人体姿态估计。

Result: 在TotalCapture数据集上MPJPE为17.2mm，在3DPW数据集上为45.3mm，均优于先前基于Transformer及其他SOTA方法；在消费级硬件上推理速度超过60 FPS。

Conclusion: VIMCAN通过混合Mamba与交叉注意力的架构设计，在保证高精度人体姿态估计的同时实现了实时推理能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: The rapid advances in deep learning have significantly enhanced the accuracy of multimodal 3D human pose estimation (HPE). However, the state-of-the-art (SOTA) HPE pipelines still rely on Transformers, whose quadratic complexity makes real-time processing for long sequences impractical. Mamba addresses this issue through selective state-space modeling, enabling efficient sequence processing without sacrificing representational power. Nevertheless, it struggles to capture complex spatial dependencies in multimodal settings. To bridge this gap, we propose VIMCAN, a hybrid architecture that combines the efficient sequence modeling of Mamba with the spatial reasoning of Cross-Attention, and performs robust visual-inertial fusion and human pose estimation between RGB keypoints and wearable IMU data. By leveraging Mamba's dynamic parameterization for temporal modeling and Attention for spatial dependency extraction, VIMCAN achieves superior accuracy, with mean per-joint position errors (MPJPE) of 17.2 mm on TotalCapture and 45.3 mm on 3DPW. VIMCAN outperforms prior Transformer-based and other SOTA approaches while supporting real-time inference at over 60 frames per second on consumer-grade hardware. The source code is available on GitHub.

</details>


### [76] [Dynamic Mode Decomposition along Depth in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07556)
*Nishant Suresh Aswani,Saif Eddin Jabari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现ViT的连续块可被单个线性算子K逼近，通过动态模式分解（DMD）拟合。在短跨度内（p≤4），K^p能高精度恢复中间激活，但该局部线性化在下游任务中效果不佳，最终层恒等映射基线与之竞争力相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明ViT连续块可被线性映射替代，并呈现递归计算相位。本文旨在探究这两点是否一致：ViT深度是否实现近似自主线性动力学，即是否存在一个单一算子K在连续跨度内反复应用。

Method: 使用动态模式分解（DMD）从选定的连续隐藏状态对中拟合线性算子K，并通过K^p进行p步前向预测。在四个预训练DINO ViT模型上研究正则化、秩和校准预算对稳定拟合的影响。

Result: 短跨度（p≤4）内，K^p与无约束端点映射的余弦相似度在DINOv3-H/16+上达到0.02以内，且能恢复每个跳过块的中间激活。早期截断处算子压缩至秩≪d，需极少校准数据；cls令牌最易线性化。但这些局部保真度无法迁移到下游任务。

Conclusion: ViT连续块在局部意义上确实呈现近似线性动力学，但这种线性逼近的效用局限于浅层，在深层和下游任务中恒等映射基线即具有竞争力。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that contiguous vision transformer (ViT) blocks (a) can be replaced by a linear map and (b) organize into recurrent phases of computation. We ask whether these observations coincide: does ViT depth implement approximately \textit{autonomous linear} dynamics, admitting a single operator $K$ applied recurrently across a contiguous span? We test this using Dynamic Mode Decomposition (DMD), which fits $K$ from selected, consecutive hidden-state pairs and predicts $p$ steps ahead via $K^p$. On four pretrained DINO ViTs, we study the regularization, rank, and calibration budget required for stable fitting. For short spans ($p \leq 4$), $K^p$ tracks an unconstrained endpoint map to within $0.02$ cosine similarity on DINOv3-H/16+, while also recovering intermediate activations at each skipped block. At early cut starts, the fitted operators compress to rank $\ll d$ with minimal calibration data, and across tokens, \texttt{cls} is most amenable to linearization; both properties decay monotonically with depth. Yet this local fidelity does not transfer downstream. At the final hidden state, after propagating through the remaining blocks, an identity baseline becomes competitive.

</details>


### [77] [Tracing the Arrow of Time: Diagnosing Temporal Information Flow in Video-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07568)
*Peitao Han,Fei Cheng,Lis K. Pereira,Qianying Liu,Shigeru Kitazawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究视频大语言模型在时序方向判断（Arrow-of-Time）任务中的表现瓶颈。通过解耦视觉编码器与Video-LLM，发现视频中心编码器能编码强时序信号，但经标准架构传递后性能崩溃。核心瓶颈在于投影器设计：Q-Former破坏时序信息，而时序保持的MLP投影能有效改善。基于此构建的Video-LLM在AoT任务上达98.1%准确率，超越人类水平，并在其他时序推理任务上取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管人类在判断视频正反播放（Arrow-of-Time）任务上近乎完美，前沿Video-LLM却仅略高于随机水平。这一差距促使研究者探究信息瓶颈究竟位于视觉编码器（是否未编码时序信息）还是Video-LLM架构的其他部分。

Method: 1) 将视觉编码器与Video-LLM解耦，分别追踪时序信息在编码器、投影器、LLM中的流动。2) 对比视频中心编码器（含显式时序建模）与帧中心编码器。3) 分析不同投影器设计（Q-Former vs. MLP）对时序信息保真度的影响。4) 进行层级的时序表征动态分析。5) 基于发现构建含时序感知编码器、时序保持投影器、AoT监督的Video-LLM。

Result: 视频中心编码器能编码强时序信号，但通过标准Video-LLM架构传输后性能崩溃。Q-Former破坏时序信息，时序保持MLP投影效果显著更好。构建的模型在AoT_PPB上达98.1%准确率（超越人类），在VITATECS-Direction上提升6.0点，在TVBench上提升1.3点。

Conclusion: 时序推理在Video-LLM中需要同时具备有效的时序编码和可靠的信息传递至LLM。投影器设计是时序信息流的关键瓶颈，时序保持的投影方案能显著改善下游时序推理能力。

Abstract: The Arrow-of-Time (AoT) task, determining whether a video plays forward or backward by recognizing temporal irreversibility, is one humans solve with near-perfect accuracy, yet frontier Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs) perform only modestly above chance. This gap raises a key question: do visual backbones fail to encode temporal information, or does information bottleneck lie elsewhere in the Video-LLM architecture? We address this question by isolating the vision encoder from the Video-LLM and tracing temporal information across the encoder, projector, and LLM. We find that video-centric encoders with explicit temporal modeling encode strong temporal signals, whereas frame-centric encoders do not. However, when video-centric representations are passed through a standard Video-LLM architecture, performance often collapses, revealing a bottleneck of temporal information flow. We identify projector design as a key factor: Q-Former disrupts temporal information, while a time-preserved MLP projection substantially improves the LLM's access to such information. Our layer-wise analysis further shows temporal representation dynamics across encoder layers. Guided by these findings, we build a Video-LLM with temporal-aware video-centric encoder, time-preserved projector, and AoT supervision, surpassing human performance on AoT$_{PPB}$ with 98.1\% accuracy, and improving broader temporal reasoning tasks by up to 6.0 points on VITATECS-Direction and 1.3 points on TVBench. Our results show that temporal reasoning in Video-LLMs requires both effective temporal encoding and reliable transfer of this information to the LLM.

</details>


### [78] [PolarVLM: Bridging the Semantic-Physical Gap in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07574)
*Yuliang Li,Chu Zhou,Heng Guo,Boxin Shi,Imari Sato,Zhanyu Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PolarVLM是首个将偏振物理参数集成到视觉-语言模型中的多模态框架，通过双流架构和两阶段训练策略，实现对反射和透明场景的物理感知语义理解，在五个评估任务上平均超越RGB基线25.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流VLM在处理反射和透明物体等严重光学模糊场景时表现不佳，这是因为标准RGB输入存在固有局限性。虽然偏振成像可以捕获解决这些模糊性的物理参数，但现有方法受限于固定格式输出，无法进行开放式推理。因此需要弥合语义与物理之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出PolarVLM框架，采用双流架构分别处理RGB和偏振物理参数，设计渐进式两阶段训练策略，在保留通用视觉能力的同时避免物理参数误解。同时构建了PolarVQA基准数据集，包含75K面向反射和透明场景的物理基础指令微调对。

Result: 在五个评估任务上，PolarVLM整体超越RGB基线25.4%，其中反射识别任务提升26.6%，玻璃计数任务提升34.0%，成功实现了物理感知的语义理解。

Conclusion: 将偏振物理参数集成到VLM中能够有效突破传统RGB输入的局限性，显著提升模型在光学模糊场景下的理解能力，为物理感知的多模态人工智能开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Mainstream vision-language models (VLMs) fundamentally struggle with severe optical ambiguities, such as reflections and transparent objects, due to the inherent limitations of standard RGB inputs. While polarization imaging captures polarimetric physical parameters that resolve these ambiguities, existing methods are constrained by fixed-format outputs and remain isolated from open-ended reasoning. To bridge this semantic-physical gap, we introduce PolarVLM, the first multimodal framework integrating polarimetric physical parameters into VLMs. By employing a dual-stream architecture and a progressive two-stage training strategy, PolarVLM effectively prevents physical misinterpretations while preserving general visual abilities. Complementing our architecture, we construct PolarVQA, the first benchmark for polarization-aware VQA, featuring 75K physics-grounded instruction-tuning pairs targeting reflective and transparent scenes. Experiments show that PolarVLM surpasses the RGB baseline by 25.4% overall across five evaluation tasks, with remarkable gains of 26.6% in reflection recognition and 34.0% in glass counting, successfully unlocking physics-aware semantic understanding.

</details>


### [79] [Response-G1: Explicit Scene Graph Modeling for Proactive Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07575)
*Ke Ma,Jiaqi Tang,Bin Guo,Xueting Han,Ruonan Xu,Qingfeng He,Ziheng Wang,Xu Wang,Qifeng Chen,Zhiwen Yu,Yunhao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Response-G1是一个面向流式视频理解的框架，通过场景图建立视频证据与查询响应条件之间的显式结构化对齐，实现无需微调的三阶段决策：查询引导的场景图生成、记忆检索历史场景图、检索增强的触发提示，在主动和被动任务上均取得优异效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Video-LLMs在流式视频理解中缺乏对视觉证据的显式、查询感知建模，导致无法准确判断何时应响应视频内容。

Method: 三阶段无需微调的框架：(1) 在线查询引导的流式片段场景图生成；(2) 基于记忆的最相关历史场景图检索；(3) 检索增强的触发提示，逐帧判断"沉默/响应"决策。

Result: 在已建立的基准测试上，所提方法在主动和被动任务中均优于现有方法，验证了显式场景图建模和检索在流式视频理解中的优势。

Conclusion: 通过将证据和条件对齐到共享的图表示，Response-G1实现了更可解释和准确的响应时序决策，为流式视频理解提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Proactive streaming video understanding requires Video-LLMs to decide when to respond as a video unfolds, a task where existing methods often fall short due to their implicit, query-agnostic modeling of visual evidence. We introduce Response-G1, a novel framework that establishes explicit, structured alignment between the accumulated video evidence and the query's expected response conditions via scene graphs. The framework operates in three fine-tuning-free stages: (1) online query-guided scene graph generation from streaming clips; (2) memory-based retrieval of the most semantically relevant historical scene graphs; and (3) retrieval-augmented trigger prompting for per-frame "silence/response" decisions.By grounding both evidence and conditions in a shared graph representation, Response-G1 achieves more interpretable and accurate response timing decisions. Experimental results on established benchmarks demonstrate the superiority of our method in both proactive and reactive tasks, validating the advantage of explicit scene graph modeling and retrieval in streaming video understanding.

</details>


### [80] [TraceAV-Bench: Benchmarking Multi-Hop Trajectory Reasoning over Long Audio-Visual Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07593)
*Hengyi Feng,Hao Liang,Mingrui Chen,Bohan Zeng,Meiyi Qiang,Zhengyang Zhao,Zimo Meng,Zeang Sheng,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TraceAV-Bench是首个联合评估长音频-视觉轨迹多跳推理和多模态幻觉鲁棒性的基准，包含2,200道题、578个长视频（总计339.5小时），平均推理链长3.68跳、时间跨度15.1分钟。最强闭源模型Gemini 3.1 Pro仅达68.29%，最佳开源模型Ming-Flash-Omni-2.0仅达51.70%，多模态幻觉鲁棒性与通用推理能力基本解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅评估短片段、隔离模态或单跳感知，无法衡量真实世界中需要对稀疏、时间分散且跨视觉和听觉线索进行链式推理的能力，缺乏同时评估长音视频轨迹多跳推理和幻觉鲁棒性的基准。

Method: 构建包含2,200道选择题的TraceAV-Bench，覆盖578个长视频（339.5小时），4个评估维度、15个子任务。每道题有显式推理链（平均3.68跳、时间跨度15.1分钟）。采用三步半自动化流水线加严格质量保证流程构建。在多个代表性OmniLLM上进行评估。

Result: 最强闭源模型Gemini 3.1 Pro在通用任务上仅达68.29%，最佳开源模型Ming-Flash-Omni-2.0达51.70%。多模态幻觉鲁棒性与通用多模态推理性能基本解耦。

Conclusion: TraceAV-Bench对现有OmniLLM构成持续挑战，仍有大量提升空间；多模态幻觉鲁棒性与通用推理能力是相对独立的能力维度。该基准将推动长音视频内容上连贯且忠实推理的OmniLLM研究。

Abstract: Real-world audio-visual understanding requires chaining evidence that is sparse, temporally dispersed, and split across the visual and auditory streams, whereas existing benchmarks largely fail to evaluate this capability. They restrict videos to short clips, isolate modalities, or reduce questions to one-hop perception. We introduce TraceAV-Bench, the first benchmark to jointly evaluate multi-hop reasoning over long audio-visual trajectories and multimodal hallucination robustness. TraceAV-Bench comprises 2,200 rigorously validated multiple-choice questions over 578 long videos, totaling 339.5 hours, spanning 4 evaluation dimensions and 15 sub-tasks. Each question is grounded in an explicit reasoning chain that averages 3.68 hops across a 15.1-minute temporal span. The dataset is built by a three-step semi-automated pipeline followed by a strict quality assurance process. Evaluation of multiple representative OmniLLMs on TraceAV-Bench reveals that the benchmark poses a persistent challenge across all models, with the strongest closed-source model (Gemini 3.1 Pro) reaching only 68.29% on general tasks, and the best open-source model (Ming-Flash-Omni-2.0) reaching 51.70%, leaving substantial headroom. Moreover, we find that robustness to multimodal hallucination is largely decoupled from general multimodal reasoning performance. We anticipate that TraceAV-Bench will stimulate further research toward OmniLLMs that can reason coherently and faithfully over long-form audio-visual content.

</details>


### [81] [LithoBench: Benchmarking Large Multimodal Models for Remote-Sensing Lithology Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07640)
*Jun Wang,Fengpeng Li,Hang Dong,Tianjin Huang,Wei Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LithoBench——一个面向遥感岩性解释中地质语义理解评估的多层级基准，包含10,000条专家标注样本、12类岩性、5个认知层级（识别描述、比较分析、机理解释、实际应用、综合推理），并设计了专家参与的半自动构建流程。实验表明现有大视觉语言模型在地质语义理解，尤其是高阶推理任务上存在显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感岩性解释是知识密集型任务，现有大模型评估缺乏包含岩性标注、多层级地质语义和专家评估的基准，亟需一个系统化的评估框架来检验模型在地质语义理解上的真实能力。

Method: 构建了含10,000条专家标注的LithoBench基准，覆盖12类岩性；设计4,000道选择题和6,000道开放题，分属5个认知层级；开发了专家在环、知识引导的半自动构建管线，包含结构化地质图像描述等子流程。

Result: 多款大视觉语言模型在地质语义理解上表现有限，尤其在高阶的解释、应用和推理任务上存在明显不足。

Conclusion: LithoBench揭示了现有模型在高阶地质语义理解上的严重短板，为遥感岩性解释的自动化评估提供了标准化基准，指明了未来模型改进的方向。

Abstract: Remote sensing lithology interpretation is fundamental to geological surveys, mineral exploration, and regional geological mapping. Unlike general land-cover recognition, lithology interpretation is a knowledge-intensive task that requires experts to infer rock types from various features, e.g., subtle visual, spectral, textural, geomorphological, and contextual cues, making reliable automated interpretation highly challenging. Geological knowledge-guided large multimodal models offer new opportunities, yet their evaluation remains constrained by the lack of benchmarks that capture lithological annotations, multi-level geological semantics, and expert-informed assessment. Here, we propose LithoBench, a multi-level benchmark for evaluating geological semantic understanding in remote sensing lithology interpretation. LithoBench contains 10,000 expert-annotated interpretation instances across 12 representative lithological categories, including 4,000 multiple-choice and 6,000 open-ended tasks organized into five cognitive levels: Identification and Description, Comparative Analysis, Mechanism Explanation, Practical Application, and Comprehensive Reasoning. We further develop an expert-in-the-loop, knowledge-grounded semi-automated construction pipeline, coupling multi sub-processes, e.g., structured geological image descriptions, to enhance geological validity and evaluation reliability. Experiments with multiple large vision-language models eveal substantial limitations in geological semantic understanding, particularly on higher-order explanation, application, and reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [82] [EggHand: A Multimodal Foundation Model for Egocentric Hand Pose Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07642)
*Jaeyoung Choi,Hyeondong Kim,Yujin Kim,Daehee Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EggHand是一个基于基础模型的框架，用于从自我中心视频预测未来3D手部姿态序列，通过将视觉-语言-动作模型中的动作解码器与自我中心视频-文本编码器相结合，实现了多模态语义推理与动态运动建模的统一，在EgoExo4D数据集上达到了最先进的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心视频中的手部运动由复杂的人类意图驱动，具有高度灵巧的关节运动，且自我运动导致的视角剧烈变化使预测极具挑战性。现有方法缺乏对多模态语义和动态运动的联合建模能力。

Method: 提出EggHand框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 从VLA模型借鉴的动作解码器，用于捕捉手部运动的结构化时间动态；(2) 自我中心视频-文本编码器，通过大规模第一人称视频学习视角感知的上下文信息。两者联合实现运动、上下文和高级意图的共同推理，无需依赖身体姿态或外部跟踪。

Result: 在EgoExo4D数据集上，EggHand在预测精度上达到了最先进水平，在严重自我运动下仍保持鲁棒性，并支持基于语言任务提示的可控预测。

Conclusion: EggHand证明了统一多模态语义推理与动态运动建模是提升自我中心手部姿态预测的有效途径，为AR/VR辅助和人机交互等具身应用提供了新的技术基础。

Abstract: Forecasting future 3D hand pose sequences from egocentric video is essential for understanding human intention and enabling embodied applications such as AR/VR assistance and human-robot interaction. However, this task remains a highly challenging problem because egocentric hand motion is driven by complex human intent, exhibits highly dexterous articulations, and is observed under drastic viewpoint shifts induced by ego-motion. In this work, we introduce EggHand, a foundation-model-based framework for egocentric hand pose forecasting that unifies multimodal semantic reasoning with dynamic motion modeling. Our approach couples an action decoder from a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model, which captures the structured temporal dynamics of hand motion, with an egocentric video-text encoder that provides viewpoint-aware contextual information learned from large-scale first-person video. Together, these components overcome the brittleness of generic visual encoders under ego-motion and enable joint reasoning over motion, context, and high-level intent-without relying on body pose or external tracking. Experiments on the EgoExo4D dataset show that EggHand sets a new state of the art in forecasting accuracy, remains robust under severe ego-motion, and further enables controllable prediction via language-based task prompts. Project page: https://jyoun9.github.io/EggHand

</details>


### [83] [GazeVLM: Active Vision via Internal Attention Control for Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07817)
*Brown Ebouky,Gabriele Carrino,Niccolo Avogaro,Christoph Studer,Andrea Bartezzaghi,Mattia Rigotti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GazeVLM通过让视觉语言模型自主生成注视令牌（<LOOK>）来控制其因果注意力掩码，模拟人类主动视觉中的元认知控制，实现自上而下的注意力导向。该模型在4B参数规模下，无需外部工具或额外视觉令牌，即可在HRBench-4k和HRBench-8k上超越同类SOTA模型约4%，以及超越基于思维型图像的智能体多模态流水线超过5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视觉推理依赖于主动视觉中的元认知控制，通过自上而下的目标导向注意力动态地将中央凹聚焦导向任务相关细节，同时保持全局场景的外周感知。而现代VLM被动处理视觉信息，依赖静态的大规模令牌上下文，稀释了空间推理能力并引发语言幻觉。

Method: GazeVLM是一种多模态架构，通过让VLM自主生成注视令牌（<LOOK>），建立对其自身因果注意力掩码的自上而下控制机制。该模型动态决定其焦点意图，触发持续抑制偏置以抑制无关视觉特征，实现空间选择性注意并模拟中央凹固定。局部推理完成后，偏置解除，恢复全局视图。使用定制的GRPO过程训练，奖励有效的接地能力。

Result: 4B参数的GazeVLM在HRBench-4k和HRBench-8k上，超越同类参数规模SOTA VLM近4%，超越基于思维型图像的智能体多模态流水线超过5%。

Conclusion: GazeVLM通过将元认知式的注意力资源调度直接内化到推理循环中，实现了全局空间感知与局部焦点推理之间的流畅切换，无需依赖外部工具或扩展上下文窗口，为多模态推理提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Human visual reasoning is governed by active vision, a process where metacognitive control drives top-down goal-directed attention, dynamically routing foveal focus toward task-relevant details while maintaining peripheral awareness of the global scene. In contrast, modern Vision-Language Models (VLMs) process visual information passively, relying on the static accumulation of massive token contexts that dilute spatial reasoning and induce linguistic hallucinations. Here we propose the following paradigm shift: GazeVLM, a multimodal architecture that internalizes this metacognitive oversight over its deployment of attention resources directly into the reasoning loop. By empowering the VLM to autonomously generate gaze tokens ($\texttt{<LOOK>}$), GazeVLM establishes a top-down control mechanism over its own causal attention mask. The model dynamically dictates its focal intent, triggering a continuous suppression bias that dampens irrelevant visual features, implementing spatial selective attention and simulating foveal fixation. Once local reasoning concludes, the bias lifts, seamlessly restoring the global view. This architecture enables the model to fluidly transition between global spatial awareness and localized focal reasoning without relying on external agentic contraptions like cropping tools, or inflating the context window with additional visual tokens derived from localized visual patches. Trained with a bespoke Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) procedure that rewards valid grounding, our 4B-parameter GazeVLM delivers strong high-resolution multimodal reasoning performance, surpassing state-of-the-art VLMs in its parameter class by nearly 4% and agentic multimodal pipelines built around thinking with images by more than 5% on HRBench-4k and HRBench-8k.

</details>


### [84] [Towards Billion-scale Multi-modal Biometric Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07655)
*Arka Koner,Chetan S. Naik,Lokesh Kurre,Vivek Raghavan,Barada P. Sabut,Tanusree Deb Barma,Anoop M. Namboodiri,Anil K. Jain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次详细介绍了基于开源架构的大规模多模态生物特征搜索系统Bharat ABIS，该系统处理指纹、人脸和虹膜三种模态，在包含2.2亿身份信息的画廊上进行评估，成人探针的FNIR为0.3%（FPIR为0.5%），并在单台服务器（8×NVIDIA H100 GPU, 2TB RAM）上实现了4000万画廊每秒100次搜索的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国家级的十亿级多模态生物特征数据库搜索系统面临采集、预处理、特征提取、精度、匹配速度、活体检测及特殊案例处理等多方面的极限挑战，目前缺乏对如此大规模多模态搜索系统的公开洞察。

Method: Bharat ABIS采用端到端流水线架构，分别对指纹、人脸和虹膜三种模态进行预处理（分割）、质量评估、呈现攻击检测和嵌入学习（特征提取），最终生成每人13.5KB的拼接模板，实现1:N去重搜索。

Result: 在来自印度Aadhaar数据库15.5亿记录中随机抽样的2.2亿身份的分层画廊上，成人探针的FNIR为0.3%（FPIR为0.5%）；与三个商业系统在2000万画廊上对比性能；单台服务器（8×NVIDIA H100, 2TB RAM）在4000万画廊上达到每秒100次搜索的吞吐量。

Conclusion: Bharat ABIS证明了基于开源架构构建大规模多模态生物特征搜索系统的可行性，在处理十亿级记录时兼顾了精度与效率，为国家级身份系统提供了技术参考。

Abstract: Searching a multi-biometric database of a billion records for a country-level identity system requires pushing the limits of all aspects of a biometric system, including acquisition, preprocessing, feature extraction, accuracy, matching speed, presentation attack detection, and handling of special cases (e.g., missing finger digits). This is the first paper that gives insights into such a large-scale multimodal biometric search system, called Bharat ABIS, based on open-source architectures. The end-to-end pipeline of Bharat ABIS processes fingerprint, face and iris modalities through modality-specific stages of preprocessing (segmentation), quality assessment, presentation attack detection, and learning an embedding (feature extraction), producing a concatenated template of 13.5KB per person. We present a detailed analysis of the modalities and how they are integrated to create an efficient and effective solution for 1:N search (de-duplication). Evaluations on a demographically stratified gallery of 220 million identities, randomly sampled from 1.55 billion records in India's Aadhaar database, yield an FNIR of 0.3% at an FPIR of 0.5%, for adult probes (over 18 years). We also compare the performance of Bharat ABIS against three state-of-the-art COTS systems on a 20M gallery. Our system achieves a throughput of 100 searches per second on a gallery of 40M on a single server (8xNvidia H100 GPUs, 2TB RAM).

</details>


### [85] [OphEdit: Training-Free Text-Guided Editing of Ophthalmic Surgical Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07695)
*Ritul Jangir,Arkya Jyoti Bagchi,Aiman Farooq,Mangalton Okram,Saurabh Seetaram Korgaonkar,Deepak Mishra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OphEdit 是一种无需训练的文本引导眼科手术视频编辑框架，通过二阶ODE反演管线捕获并注入注意力V张量，在保持眼部解剖结构完整性的同时实现文本驱动的语义修改，首次将无训练视频编辑应用于眼科手术领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真手术视频生成对医学培训和AI开发至关重要，但现有生成模型在精确视频编辑方面面临巨大挑战——修改手术属性（如器械-组织交互、手术阶段）受严格解剖和时间约束限制，且传统方法需要大量人工标注和昂贵的模型微调。

Method: 提出OphEdit框架，采用确定性二阶ODE反演管线从原始视频中捕获注意力值（V）张量，在去噪阶段选择性地将这些存储的张量注入条件分类器无引导（CFG）分支，从而在保持精细眼部解剖几何结构的同时实现文本驱动的语义映射编辑。

Result: 临床评估表明，OphEdit能有效处理复杂手术变换（如器械替换、程序变体），在结构保真度和时间一致性上显著优于自然域视频编辑器。

Conclusion: 这是无训练视频编辑在眼科手术领域的首个应用，为生成多样化、标注完整的医学数据集提供了可扩展的解决方案，无需繁琐的人工录制或昂贵的模型微调。

Abstract: High-fidelity surgical video generation can greatly improve medical training and the development of AI, adapting these generative models for precise video editing remains a formidable challenge. Modifying surgical attributes, such as instrument tissue interactions or procedural phases is challenging due to the strict anatomical and temporal constraints. In this paper, we propose OphEdit, a novel training-free framework for the text-guided editing of ophthalmic surgical videos. Our approach leverages a deterministic second-order ODE inversion pipeline to capture Attention Value (V) tensors from the original video. By selectively injecting these stored tensors into the conditional Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG) branch during the denoising phase, OphEdit rigorously preserves the intricate anatomical geometry of the eye while seamlessly mapping text-driven semantic modifications onto the video stream. Clinical evaluations demonstrates that OphEdit effectively handles complex surgical transformations, such as instrument swaps and procedural variations, with superior structural fidelity and temporal consistency compared to natural-domain video editors. Our work represents the first application of training-free video editing in the ophthalmic surgical domain, offering a scalable solution for generating diverse, annotated medical datasets without the need for exhaustive manual recording or costly model fine-tuning. The code and prompts can be accessed at https://github.com/ophedit/OphEdit

</details>


### [86] [Benchmarking Foundation Models for Renal Lesion Stratification in CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07749)
*Hartmut Häntze,Sarah de Boer,Myrthe Buser,Alessa Hering,Bram van Ginneken,Mathias Prokop,Jawed Nawabi,Sebastian Ziegelmayer,Lisa Adams,Keno Bressem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估了三种开源医学基础模型在CT肾病变分类任务上的迁移学习能力，发现其表现（AUC 0.70-0.77）与从头训练的3D ResNet-50（AUC 0.72）相当，但显著低于传统影像组学方法（AUC 0.88），表明当前医学基础模型尚未能捕捉到组织学亚型判别所需的细粒度纹理与形状异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源医学基础模型快速涌现，但其在数据稀缺的临床分类任务中的实际迁移能力尚不明确。CT肾病变分类因训练数据天然受限，亟需具备更强泛化能力的方法，因此系统评估医学基础模型在该任务上的表现具有现实意义。

Method: 构建包含2,854个病变的复合数据集，采用冻结特征探针协议比较三种医学基础模型的嵌入表示，与手工影像组学分类器及从头训练的3D ResNet-50进行对照，并在来自TCIA的234个病变外部测试集上评估六分类任务（包括常见病变如囊肿、透明细胞肾细胞癌及罕见亚型）。

Result: 医学基础模型AUC为0.70-0.77，与从头训练的ResNet（AUC 0.72）无显著差异，但在特征提取后仅需CPU秒级运算，硬件需求大幅降低。然而传统影像组学基线显著优于所有深度学习方法（AUC 0.88，所有p≤0.002）。

Conclusion: 当前通用医学基础模型的嵌入表示尚未能捕捉肾病变组织学亚型判别所需的细粒度纹理与形状异质性。尽管在数据稀缺场景中具有潜力，但医学基础模型在肾病变分层任务中尚未超越已建立的模型，影像组学仍是当前最优方法。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of open-source medical foundation models (FMs) raises a practical question: how well do their pre-trained representations transfer to clinically relevant but data-scarce classification tasks? Particularly in CT-based renal lesion classification, a push toward greater generalizability would be meaningful, as the field is constrained by inherently limited training data. We addressed this through a benchmark of three medical FMs on this specific task. This six-class problem spans common entities like cysts and clear cell renal cell carcinoma, alongside rare subtypes. Using a frozen feature-probing protocol, we compared FM embeddings against a handcrafted radiomics classifier and a 3D ResNet-50 trained from scratch. Models were trained on a composite dataset of 2,854 lesions and evaluated on an external test set of 234 lesions from The Cancer Imaging Archive. Our results reveal two key findings. First, FM performance (AUC 0.70-0.77) matched the from-scratch ResNet (AUC 0.72) while drastically reducing hardware demand, requiring only seconds on a CPU after feature extraction. However, the conventional radiomics baseline significantly outperformed all deep learning approaches, achieving an AUC of 0.88 (all p $\leq$ 0.002). This suggests that current generalist FM embeddings do not yet capture the fine-grained texture and shape heterogeneity driving histological subtype discrimination. Despite their potential in data-scarce settings, medical FMs did not surpass established models for renal lesion stratification, leaving radiomics as the current state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [87] [Head Similarity: Modeling Structured Whole-Head Appearance Beyond Face Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07766)
*Yingfeng Wang,Yuxuan Xiao,Shengcai Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Head Similarity（头部相似性）这一新范式，将传统身份识别扩展到结构化全头相似性建模，显式捕捉身份内表观变化并构建层次化相似性排序，在非正面视角或面部线索缺失场景下实现更有意义的相似度比较。通过大规模弱监督基准和联合建模身份判别与表观敏感相似性的框架，实验证明传统人脸识别模型无法捕捉表观依赖的相似性，而所提方法可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人脸识别模型强制身份内不变性，将发型、造型等表观变化压缩为单一表征，导致在非正面视角或面部线索缺失时无法区分同一身份的不同表观状态，限制了在表观敏感场景（如视频监控、人物追踪）中的应用。

Method: 1) 提出Head Similarity新问题定义，扩展身份识别为结构化全头相似性建模；2) 从长视频中构建大规模弱监督基准，覆盖多样姿态、遮挡和时间变化；3) 开发联合建模身份判别与表观敏感相似性的框架，通过层次化监督和身份感知蒸馏实现。

Result: 传统人脸识别模型在捕捉表观依赖相似性方面表现失败，而所提方法证明了结构化全头相似性建模的可行性。

Conclusion: Head Similarity为表观敏感场景下的身份一致性提供了新思路，超越了严格生物特征识别的局限，在遮挡、后视图等挑战条件下仍能进行有意义的相似度比较。

Abstract: Many vision applications require identity consistency beyond strict biometric recognition, especially under non-frontal views or when facial cues are missing. However, conventional face recognition models enforce intra-identity invariance, collapsing appearance variations such as hairstyle or styling changes into a single representation, limiting their use in appearance-sensitive scenarios. To address this limitation, we introduce Head Similarity, a new formulation that extends identity-centric recognition to structured whole-head similarity modeling. Our approach explicitly captures intra-identity appearance variation and enforces hierarchical similarity ordering across identity and appearance states, enabling meaningful comparison even under occlusion or rear-view conditions. We construct a large-scale benchmark from long-form videos with weakly-supervised appearance states, covering diverse poses, occlusions, and temporal changes. As a first step, we develop a simple yet effective framework that jointly models identity discrimination and appearance-sensitive similarity through hierarchical supervision and identity-aware distillation. Experiments show that conventional face recognition models fail to capture appearance-dependent similarity, while our approach demonstrates the feasibility of structured whole-head similarity modeling.

</details>


### [88] [Differentiable Ray Tracing with Gaussians for Unified Radio Propagation Simulation and View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07781)
*Niklas Vaara,Lam Huynh,Pekka Sangi,Miguel Bordallo López,Janne Heikkilä*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种框架，利用3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）在神经重建场景中实现可微分的射频（RF）传播模拟，使得无需手动构建网格，即可在任意3D位置间计算点对点路径，同时保持高质量视觉渲染，证明了神经重建可作为电磁传播仿真与逼真视图合成的统一空间表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的显式神经表示（如3DGS）虽能实现高保真、实时的新视角合成，但其优化目标基于阿尔法混合的光学外观，而非支持光线求交的几何结构。而射频数字孪生需要确定性的多跳路径，几何结构决定轨迹、衰减和延迟。传统RF仿真依赖手工构建网格，缺乏与视觉重建的兼容性。因此，需要一种能在视觉重建场景中直接进行可微分RF传播模拟的方法。

Method: 将3D高斯图元嵌入到硬件加速的光线追踪结构中作为底层空间表示，在视觉重建的神经场景中实现可微分的RF传播模拟，支持任意3D位置间的点对点路径计算，同时保留高质量的视觉渲染能力。

Result: 从仅视觉重建中提取了具有物理意义的信道冲激响应，提供了跨模态证据，表明神经重建可以作为电磁传播仿真与逼真视图合成的统一空间表示。

Conclusion: 神经重建（如3DGS）不仅是视觉渲染的有效表示，还可以作为射频传播模拟的统一空间表示，弥合了视觉与电磁仿真之间的鸿沟，为无线数字孪生等应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Explicit neural representations such as 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enable high-fidelity and real-time novel view synthesis, yet optimize for alpha-composited optical appearance rather than ray-intersectable geometry. In contrast, radio-frequency (RF) digital twins require deterministic multi-bounce paths, where the geometry dictates trajectories and their associated attenuation and delay. We introduce a framework enabling differentiable RF propagation simulation directly within visually reconstructed neural scenes, allowing point-to-point path computation between arbitrary 3D locations while preserving high-quality visual rendering. Unlike conventional RF simulation pipelines that rely on manually constructed meshes, we embed Gaussian primitives into a hardware-accelerated ray tracing structure as the underlying spatial representation. By extracting physically meaningful channel impulse responses from visual-only reconstructions, we provide cross-modal evidence that neural reconstructions can serve as unified spatial representations for both electromagnetic propagation simulation and photorealistic view synthesis.

</details>


### [89] [Radiologist-Guided Causal Concept Bottleneck Models for Chest X-Ray Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07785)
*Amy Rafferty,Rishi Ramaesh,Ajitha Rajan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出XpertCausal，一种放射科医生引导的因果概念瓶颈模型，用于胸部X光片解读。该方法采用概率性noisy-OR框架建模病理到概念的关系，并通过贝叶斯推理从预测概念中估计病理概率，同时利用放射科医生 curated 的概念-病理关联约束模型结构。在MIMIC-CXR数据集上，XpertCausal相比非因果CBM基线及因果消融模型，在AUROC、校准度、解释质量和临床推理路径对齐方面均取得更优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学影像概念瓶颈模型（CBMs）通常将概念视为病理标签的判别式预测器，而未显式建模疾病产生可观察影像学发现这一潜在的临床生成过程，导致模型缺乏临床可解释性和推理路径的合理性。

Method: 使用概率性noisy-OR框架建模病理到概念之间的因果关系，并通过贝叶斯推理反演该生成模型，从预测概念中估计病理概率；同时引入放射科医生 curated 的概念-病理关联来约束模型结构，使其遵循临床合理的推理路径。

Result: 在MIMIC-CXR数据集上，XpertCausal在病理分类AUROC、校准度、解释质量以及专家知识对齐度上均优于非因果CBM基线和取消了专家约束的因果消融模型。

Conclusion: 将临床启发的因果结构和专家领域知识融入概念瓶颈模型，能够构建更准确、可解释且与临床对齐的胸部X光片解读模型。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) in medical imaging aim to improve model interpretability by predicting intermediate clinical concepts before final diagnoses. However, most existing CBMs treat concepts as discriminative predictors of pathology labels, without explicitly modelling the underlying clinical generative process where diseases produce observable radiographic findings. We propose XpertCausal, a radiologist-guided causal CBM for chest X-ray interpretation which models pathology-to-concept relationships using a probabilistic noisy-OR framework. This generative model is then inverted via Bayesian inference to estimate pathology probabilities from predicted concepts. Radiologist-curated concept-pathology associations are used to constrain model structure to radiologist-defined clinically plausible reasoning pathways. We evaluate XpertCausal on MIMIC-CXR across pathology classification performance, calibration, explanation quality, and alignment with radiologist-defined reasoning pathways. Compared with both a non-causal CBM baseline and a causal ablation with unconstrained learned associations, XpertCausal achieves improved AUROC, calibration, and clinically relevant explanation quality, while learning concept-pathology relationships that more closely align with expert knowledge. These results demonstrate that incorporating clinically motivated causal structure and expert domain knowledge into CBMs can lead to more accurate, interpretable, and clinically aligned models for CXR interpretation.

</details>


### [90] [APEX: Assumption-free Projection-based Embedding eXamination Metric for Image Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07786)
*Caterina Gallegati,Monica Bianchini,Franco Scarselli,Vittorio Murino,Barbara Toniella Corradini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出APEX评估框架，利用切片Wasserstein距离替代传统参数化指标，结合CLIP和DINOv2作为开放词汇特征提取器，在视觉质量评估中展现出更强的鲁棒性和跨数据集稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像评估指标（如FID）受限于闭集词汇瓶颈和刚性参数化假设，而现有替代方案虽解决了特征瓶颈但仍受参数化局限，因此需要一种无假设、嵌入无关的评估框架。

Method: 提出APEX框架，核心采用切片Wasserstein距离作为无假设的相似性度量，理论证明其在高维空间的可扩展性，并使用CLIP和DINOv2两个开放词汇基础模型作为特征提取器，实现嵌入无关性。

Result: APEX在鲁棒性基准测试中优于既有基线，对视觉退化表现出更强的鲁棒性；同时展现出良好的数据集内和跨数据集稳定性，在域外数据集上能保持高度稳定的评估结果。

Conclusion: APEX通过无参数假设的数学度量结合开放词汇特征，有效克服了传统指标的两大瓶颈，为生成模型评估提供了更可靠、更稳定的替代方案。

Abstract: As generative models achieve unprecedented visual quality, the gold standard for image evaluation remains traditional feature-distribution metrics (e.g., FID). However, these metrics are provably hindered by the closed-vocabulary bottleneck of outdated features and the assumptive bias of rigid parametric formulations. Recent alternatives exploit modern backbones to solve the feature bottleneck, yet continue to suffer from parametric limitations. To close this gap, we introduce APEX (Assumption-free Projection-based Embedding eXamination), a novel evaluation framework leveraging the Sliced Wasserstein Distance as a mathematically grounded, assumption-free similarity measure. APEX inherits effective scalability to high-dimensional spaces, as we prove with theoretical and empirical evidences. Moreover, APEX is embedding-agnostic and uses two open-vocabulary foundation models, CLIP and DINOv2, as feature extractors. Benchmarking APEX against established baselines reveals superior robustness to visual degradations. Additionally, we show that APEX metrics exhibit intra- and cross-dataset stability, ensuring highly stable evaluations on out-of-domain datasets.

</details>


### [91] [SARA: Semantically Adaptive Relational Alignment for Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07800)
*Jiesong Lian,Zixiang Zhou,Ruizhe Zhong,Yuan Zhou,Qinglin Lu,Rui Wang,Long Hu,Yixue Hao,Baoru Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SARA通过文本条件化的语义显著性指导token关系蒸馏，在冻结的视觉基础模型上实现对视频扩散模型的细粒度文本对齐改进，在Wan2.2持续训练中优于SFT、VideoREPA和MoAlign。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型在实体保持、属性绑定和交互描述方面仍存在缺陷，而VideoREPA和MoAlign等表征对齐方法虽能改进细粒度文本遵循，但其监督预算由视觉或运动线索分配，而非关注每个token对与提示的相关性。

Method: SARA包含两个阶段：Stage 1训练一个轻量级对齐器，使用SAM 3.1的逐实体掩码监督和InfoNCE正则化；将文本条件化的连续显著性通过pair-routing算子融合到token关系蒸馏中，为每个token对分配权重，将监督导向主体-主体和主体-背景对，远离背景-背景对。

Result: 在Wan2.2持续训练设置下，SARA在13维VLM评估标准、VBench基准和盲测用户研究中，在文本对齐和运动质量上均优于SFT、VideoREPA和MoAlign。

Conclusion: SARA通过语义自适应的关系对齐策略，有效提升了视频扩散模型的细粒度文本遵循能力，证明了文本条件化显著性引导蒸馏的优越性。

Abstract: Recent video diffusion models (VDMs) synthesize visually convincing clips, yet still drop entities, mis-bind attributes, and weaken the interactions specified in the prompt. Representation-alignment objectives such as VideoREPA and MoAlign improve fine-grained text following by distilling spatio-temporal token relations from a frozen visual foundation model, but their pairwise supervision budget is allocated by visual or motion cues rather than by how relevant each pair is to the prompt. We present SARA, Semantically Adaptive Relational Alignment, which keeps token-relation distillation (TRD) on a frozen VFM target and adds a text-conditioned saliency that decides which token pairs carry supervision. A lightweight Stage 1 aligner is trained with per-entity SAM 3.1 mask supervision and an InfoNCE regulariser, and its continuous saliency is fused into TRD through a pair-routing operator that assigns each token pair a weight whenever either of its two endpoints is salient, thereby routing supervision toward subject-subject and subject-background pairs and away from background-background ones. In the Wan2.2 continual-training setting, SARA improves both text alignment and motion quality over SFT, VideoREPA, and MoAlign on a 13-dimension VLM rubric, on the public VBench benchmarks, and in a blind user study.

</details>


### [92] [Text-to-CAD Evaluation with CADTests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07807)
*Dimitrios Mallis,Marco Wang,Ahmet Serdar Karadeniz,Elisa Ricci,Anis Kacem,Djamila Aouada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CADTestBench，首个基于自动化测试的Text-to-CAD评估基准，通过可执行的CADTests验证生成模型是否满足几何和拓扑要求，并展示了该基准在模型评估和生成引导两方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Text-to-CAD领域缺乏系统化的评估方法，现有工作难以有效衡量CAD模型生成的质量，亟需一种客观、可复现的评估标准。

Method: 提出CADTestBench基准，核心是CADTests——一组可执行的软件测试，用于验证生成CAD模型是否满足输入文本中的几何和拓扑约束；并利用该基准对现有Text-to-CAD方法进行全面对比，同时探索将CADTests作为生成指导信号来改进模型性能。

Result: 基于CADTestBench对当前Text-to-CAD方法进行了系统评估，并证明利用CADTests作为生成指导可以构建简单基线，其性能超越现有方法。

Conclusion: CADTestBench为Text-to-CAD提供了首个可执行的测试驱动评估方案，既可用于公平评测，也可用于提升生成质量，代码和数据集已开源。

Abstract: Text-to-CAD has recently emerged as an important task with the potential to substantially accelerate design workflows. Despite its significance, there has been surprisingly little work on Text-to-CAD evaluation, and assessing CAD model generation performance remains a considerable challenge. In this work, we introduce a new evaluation perspective for Text-to-CAD based on automated testing. We propose CADTestBench, the first test-based benchmark for Text-to-CAD, based on CADTests, executable software tests that verify whether a generated CAD model satisfies the geometric and topological requirements of the input prompt. Using CADTestBench, we conduct comprehensive benchmarking of recent Text-to-CAD methods and further demonstrate that CADTests can also guide CAD model generation, yielding simple baselines that surpass performance of current methods. CADTestBench code and data are available at GitHub and Hugging Face dataset.

</details>


### [93] [ICDAR 2026 Competition on Writer Identification and Pen Classification from Hand-Drawn Circles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07816)
*Thomas Gorges,Janne van der Loop,Lukas Hüttner,Linda-Sophie Schneider,Fei Wu,Mathias Seuret,Vincent Christlein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CircleID——ICDAR 2026大型竞赛，旨在从扫描手绘圆圈中进行书写者识别和笔分类。竞赛包含两个任务：开放集书写者识别和跨书写者笔分类。数据集包含46,155个紧密裁剪的圆圈图像，来自50位已知和16位未知书写者使用8种不同笔。最终提交中，书写者识别Top-1准确率达64.801%，笔分类达92.726%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究生物特征（书写者特性）与物理特征（笔类型）如何在最小、静态的轨迹中自然纠缠，并建立最小轨迹分析的新基准。

Method: 构建了包含46,155张400 DPI扫描圆圈图像的控制数据集，来自66位书写者（50已知+16未知）使用8种不同笔。竞赛在Kaggle上分为两个赛道，提供ResNet基线模型，分别进行开放集书写者识别和跨书写者笔分类。

Result: 389个团队（436名参与者）参与笔分类任务，提交3,185次；113个团队（141名参与者）参与书写者识别任务，提交1,737次。最佳私有排行榜结果：书写者识别Top-1准确率64.801%，笔分类Top-1准确率92.726%。

Conclusion: CircleID为最小轨迹分析建立了新基准，并揭示了分布外书写者对模型泛化能力和特征解耦的影响。

Abstract: This paper presents CircleID, a large-scale ICDAR 2026 competition on writer identification and pen classification from scanned hand-drawn circles. The primary objective is to investigate how biometric writer characteristics and physical pen features naturally entangle within minimal, static traces. CircleID comprises two distinct tasks: (1) open-set writer identification, requiring models to recognize known writers while explicitly rejecting unknown ones, and (2) cross-writer pen classification, evaluated across both seen and unseen writers. Participants were provided with a new, controlled dataset of 46,155 tightly cropped circle images, digitized at 400 DPI and annotated for writer identity and pen type. The dataset comprises samples from 50 known and 16 unknown writers using eight different pens. Hosted on Kaggle as two separate tracks with public and private leaderboards, the competition provided participants with a ResNet baseline. In total, 389 teams (436 participants) made 3,185 submissions for the pen classification task, and 113 teams (141 participants) made 1,737 submissions for the writer identification track. The best-performing private leaderboard submissions achieved a Top-1 accuracy of 64.801% for writer identification and 92.726% for pen classification. This paper details the dataset, evaluates the winning methodologies, and analyzes the impact of out-of-distribution writers on model generalization and feature disentanglement. In this large-scale competition, CircleID establishes a new baseline for minimal-trace analysis.

</details>


### [94] [Divide and Conquer: Object Co-occurrence Helps Mitigate Simplicity Bias in OOD Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07821)
*Boyang Dai,Chaoqi Chen,Yizhou Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于物体共现模式(OCO)的OOD检测框架，通过解耦表示预测物体共现模式，采用分治策略处理三种场景，在挑战性OOD设置下取得了竞争性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD检测方法多关注常规纠缠表示来区分ID和OOD数据，忽视了图像中丰富的上下文信息，尤其在检测近OOD时因模型简单性偏差而难以学习解耦表示中的判别特征。

Method: 提出OCO框架：1) 通过预测测试样本的解耦表示来理解图像中的物体共现模式；2) 基于ID训练数据中的物体共现模式自适应地将模式划分为三种场景；3) 采用分治策略进行OOD检测。

Result: 在挑战性和全光谱OOD设置下进行实验，取得了竞争性结果，证实了方法处理语义偏移和协变量偏移的能力。

Conclusion: OCO通过考虑语义上下文关系来区分近OOD，避免了仅关注简单易学区域的倾向，为OOD检测提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection is crucial for ensuring the reliability of deep learning models. Existing methods mostly focus on regular entangled representations to discriminate in-distribution (ID) and OOD data, neglecting the rich contextual information within images. This issue is particularly challenging for detecting near-OOD, as models with simplicity bias struggle to learn discriminative features in disentangled representations. The human visual system can use the co-occurrence of objects in the natural environment to facilitate scene understanding. Inspired by this, we propose an Object-Centric OOD detection framework that learns to capture Object CO-occurrence (OCO) patterns within images. The proposed method introduces a new OOD detection paradigm that understands object co-occurrence within an image by predicting disentangled representations for the test sample, then adaptively divides patterns into three scenarios based on object co-occurrence patterns observed in ID training data, and finally performs OOD detection in a divide-and-conquer manner. By doing so, OCO can distinguish near-OOD by considering the semantic contextual relationships present in their images, avoiding the tendency to focus solely on simple, easily learnable regions. We evaluate OCO through experiments across challenging and full-spectrum OOD settings, demonstrating competitive results and confirming its ability to address both semantic and covariate shifts. Code is released at https://github.com/Michael-McQueen/OCO.

</details>


### [95] [Explainable Part-Based Vehicle Classifier with Spatial Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07831)
*Andreas Caduff,Klaus Zahn,Jonas Hofstetter,Martin Rechsteiner,Patrick Flaig*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于空间感知部件（Spatially-Aware Parts）的车辆分类方法，将CNN分解为部件检测器、空间概率图构建和softmax分类三个模块，在保持与端到端CNN相当精度的同时，提升了模型的可解释性和对误检测的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统端到端CNN作为黑盒模型，难以解释且扩展新类别需要完全重训练；已有部件化方法缺乏空间感知能力，对误检测鲁棒性不足。本文旨在在保持高精度的前提下提升模型的可解释性和实用性。

Method: 将标准CNN分解为三阶段：(1) CNN-based部件检测器检测语义强的车辆部件；(2) 构建全空间概率图，对每个部件相对于车辆类别的位置进行条件概率建模；(3) 采用softmax回归进行最终车辆分类。相比此前二值化特征（存在/不存在），空间概率图显著提升了鲁棒性。

Result: 该方法对部件误检测的鲁棒性大幅提升，在分类精度上与端到端CNN相当，成功挑战了精度与可解释性之间的权衡假设。

Conclusion: 该空间感知部件方法在智能交通系统中实现了高精度与可解释性的统一，为构建兼具准确性和直观可解释性的车辆分类系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: In the area of Intelligent Transportation Systems (ITS), fine-grained vehicle classification systems play an essential role. Recently, the authors have presented a novel vision-based classification approach in which standard end-to-end Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) have been decomposed into 1) a CNN-based detector for semantically strong vehicle parts, followed by 2) feature construction and 3) final classification by a decision tree. In contrast to conventional CNNs, this allows both easy extensibility to new vehicle categories - without the need to fully retrain the part detector - and an important step towards the interpretability of the model, removing partially the black-box nature inherent to CNNs. Here we present an important extension of this approach that now incorporates spatial awareness of the vehicle parts: while the feature construction 2) of the previous approach used a binary decision for each feature (present vs. absent), now a full spatial probability map is constructed to condition the presence of each individual part with respect to a given vehicle category. The classification is performed using a softmax regression approach for the overall vehicle probabilities. This method shows a considerably improved robustness against false (part-)detections, a point that is crucial for practical application. Comparative analyses with a state-of-the-art end-to-end CNN indicate that our part-based methods achieve comparable accuracy, effectively challenging the presumed trade-off between accuracy and explainability. This research represents a significant advance in vehicle classification for ITS and forms the basis for systems that combine high accuracy with intuitive interpretability.

</details>


### [96] [EyeCue: Driver Cognitive Distraction Detection via Gaze-Empowered Egocentric Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07859)
*Lang Zhang,JinYi Yoon,Matthew Corbett,Abhijit Sarkar,Bo Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EyeCue框架，利用眼动与第一人称视频结合来检测驾驶员认知分心，并构建了CogDrive多场景数据集。EyeCue通过建模视线与视觉上下文的交互，在CogDrive上达到74.38%的准确率，超越11个基线模型7%以上，且在不同驾驶场景下泛化能力强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶员认知分心是导致交通事故的主要原因，但现有方法难以检测——认知分心时驾驶员看似视觉专注且无显著身体动作，传统基于物理行为或多模态的方法效果有限。现有数据集规模小、场景单一，缺乏认知分心的标注。

Method: 提出EyeCue框架，核心思路是认知分心会体现在眼动与视觉上下文的交互中。该方法将眼动数据与第一人称视频（egocentric video）融合，实现驾驶员注意力随时间变化的上下文感知建模（context-aware modeling）。同时构建CogDrive数据集，对四个现有驾驶数据集补充认知分心标注。

Result: 在CogDrive上，EyeCue达到74.38%的最高准确率，超越6个模型家族的11个基线方法7%以上；在不同道路类型、时间段和天气条件下均能保持超70%的准确率，泛化能力良好。

Conclusion: 结果表明建模视线-上下文交互对认知分心检测至关重要，跨模态交互建模在多模态认知分心检测中具有有效性。

Abstract: Driver cognitive distraction is a major cause of road collisions and remains difficult to detect. Unlike manual or visual distraction, cognitive distraction is diverted by thoughts unrelated to driving, even when the driver appears visually attentive and exhibits no explicit physical movements. In this work, we propose EyeCue, a gaze-empowered egocentric video understanding framework, to detect driver cognitive distraction. A key insight is that cognitive distraction manifests in the interaction between eye gaze and visual context. To capture this interaction, EyeCue integrates eye gaze with egocentric video to enable context-aware modeling of the driver's attention over time. Furthermore, to tackle the limited scale and diversity of existing datasets, we introduce CogDrive, a comprehensive multi-scenario dataset that augments four existing driving datasets with cognitive distraction annotations. Through extensive evaluations on CogDrive, we show that EyeCue achieves the highest accuracy of 74.38%, outperforming 11 baselines from 6 model families by over 7%. Notably, EyeCue can achieve an accuracy of over 70% across various driving scenarios (different road types, times of day, and weather conditions) with strong generalizability. These results highlight the importance of modeling gaze-context interactions and the effectiveness of cross-modal interaction modeling for multimodal cognitive distraction detection. Our codes and CogDrive dataset resources are available at https://github.com/langzhang2000/EyeCue.

</details>


### [97] [From Synthetic to Real: Toward Identity-Consistent Makeup Transfer with Synthetic and Real Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07861)
*Yue Yu,Jiayu Wang,Jiajia Shi,Jingjing Chen,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ConsistentBeauty数据管线确保合成数据中的妆容保真度和身份一致性，以及RealBeauty合成到真实的微调框架，通过强化学习和可验证奖励函数提升模型在真实场景下的妆容迁移性能，在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散/流模型的妆容迁移方法存在两个关键挑战：合成监督信号难以忠实保持源人物身份，以及合成数据与真实数据之间的域差距限制了模型在复杂真实场景中的泛化能力。

Method: 1) ConsistentBeauty：新颖的数据管线，保证合成数据中妆容保真度和严格的身份一致性；2) RealBeauty：合成到真实的训练后微调框架，在合成数据监督学习基础上，通过强化学习并设计针对妆容迁移任务的可验证奖励函数，使模型能从真实妆容模式中进一步受益。

Result: 在多个基准上达到SOTA性能，在身份保留和复杂真实场景下表现具有明显优势，并建立了涵盖多种肤色、年龄、性别、姿势和妆容风格的新基准。

Conclusion: 通过数据管线和强化学习微调相结合，有效解决了合成监督中身份保持不足和域差距问题，显著提升了妆容迁移在真实场景中的泛化能力。

Abstract: Makeup transfer aims to apply the makeup style of a reference portrait to a source portrait while preserving identity and background. Early methods formulate this task as unsupervised image-to-image translation, relying on surrogate objectives and often yielding limited performance. Recent diffusion- and flow-based approaches instead exploit synthetic data for supervised training, leading to significant improvements. However, these methods still face two critical challenges: synthetic supervision frequently fails to faithfully preserve identity, and the domain gap between synthetic and real data limits generalization, resulting in degraded performance in complex real-world scenarios. To address these issues, this paper first proposes ConsistentBeauty, a novel data curation pipeline that ensures makeup fidelity and strict identity consistency within the synthesized data. Second, we propose RealBeauty, a synthetic-to-real post-training framework. Beyond supervised learning on curated synthetic data, we further adapt the model to real-world scenarios through reinforcement learning and design novel verifiable rewards tailored to the makeup transfer task. It allows the model to further benefit from real makeup patterns beyond synthetic supervision. In addition, we establish a new diverse benchmark for makeup transfer, covering a wide range of skin tones, ages, genders, poses, and makeup styles, thereby enabling a more comprehensive evaluation of model performance under diverse real-world conditions. Extensive experiments show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple benchmarks and demonstrates clear advantages in identity preservation and performance on complex real-world cases.

</details>


### [98] [Video Understanding Reward Modeling: A Robust Benchmark and Performant Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07872)
*Yuancheng Wei,Linli Yao,Lei Li,Haojie Zhang,Hao Zhou,Fandong Meng,Xu Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的框架，涵盖基准设计、数据构建和奖励模型训练，以推动视频理解中的奖励建模。具体包括：VURB基准（2,100个偏好对，含长链思维推理轨迹）、VUP-35K数据集（自动化管道构建的大规模高质量偏好数据），以及在此基础上训练的两个SOTA奖励模型VideoDRM（判别式）和VideoGRM（生成式）。实验表明，所提方法在VURB和VideoRewardBench上均达到最优，VUP-35K还能提升奖励性能和模型推理能力，并在测试时扩展中带来显著收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频理解奖励建模严重缺乏鲁棒的评估基准和高质量的偏好数据，这限制了该领域的进展。

Method: 提出统一框架：(1) VURB基准：2,100个偏好对，含平均1,143 tokens的长链思维推理轨迹，覆盖通用、长视频和推理型任务，采用多数投票评估；(2) VUP-35K数据集：通过全自动管道构建的大规模高质量偏好数据；(3) 基于这些数据训练判别式奖励模型VideoDRM和生成式奖励模型VideoGRM。

Result: VideoDRM和VideoGRM在VURB和VideoRewardBench上均达到SOTA性能；VUP-35K同时提升了奖励性能和模型推理能力；VideoDRM和VideoGRM在best-of-N测试时扩展中带来了显著增益。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一框架有效解决了视频理解奖励建模中基准和数据缺乏的问题，所训练的奖励模型在多项评估中达到最优，并验证了大规模偏好数据对模型能力的提升作用。

Abstract: Multimodal reward models have advanced substantially in text and image domains, yet progress in video understanding reward modeling remains severely limited by the lack of robust evaluation benchmarks and high-quality preference data. To address this, we propose a unified framework spanning benchmark design, data construction, and reward model training. We introduce Video Understanding Reward Bench (VURB), a benchmark featuring 2,100 preference pairs with long chain-of-thought reasoning traces (averaging 1,143 tokens) and majority voting evaluation across general, long, and reasoning-oriented video tasks. We further construct Video Understanding Preference Dataset (VUP-35K) via a fully automated pipeline, providing large-scale high-quality supervision for video reward training. Building on the data, we train VideoDRM and VideoGRM, a discriminative and a generative reward model, both achieving state-of-the-art performance on VURB and VideoRewardBench. Further analysis confirms that VUP-35K enhances both reward performance and model reasoning capability, while VideoDRM and VideoGRM yield significant gains under best-of-$N$ test-time scaling.

</details>


### [99] [Semantic-Aware Adaptive Visual Memory for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07897)
*Hang Wu,Sherin Mary Mathews,Yujun Cai,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Yiwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAVEMem 是一个无需训练的双阶段框架，通过语义感知的流式内存管理和查询自适应检索，在保持恒定内存预算的同时显著提升在线流视频理解性能，并降低峰值GPU内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线流视频理解中，无界的视频流和不可预测的查询时机使得内存管理成为核心挑战。现有方法通常基于视觉相似性启发式压缩视觉token，或在其后增加KV缓存检索，但压缩决策缺乏语义信号，且检索与压缩难以协调。

Method: 提出SAVEMem双阶段框架：（1）构建三层流式内存，利用固定伪问题库提供轻量级语义先验，使长期保留由语义显著性而非仅视觉相似性决定；（2）执行查询感知检索，通过锚定条件的时间门控自适应调整检索范围（短期/中期/长期），并在此范围内通过查询与内存token的后期交互选择候选帧。

Result: 将Qwen2.5-VL在OVO-Bench总体得分从52.27提升至62.69，在StreamingBench和ODV-Bench上取得一致改进，同时在128帧设置下比基线降低48%的峰值GPU内存。

Conclusion: 将语义感知引入流式视频内存管理，并通过查询自适应检索实现压缩与检索的协调，在不训练的条件下显著提升在线视频理解性能与内存效率。

Abstract: Online streaming video understanding requires models to process continuous visual inputs and respond to user queries in real time, where the unbounded stream and unpredictable query timing turn memory management into a central challenge. Existing methods typically compress visual tokens via visual similarity heuristics, or augment compression with KV-cache-level retrieval. However, compression decisions rarely incorporate semantic signals, and retrieval is often added after compression is finalized, making the two stages hard to coordinate. We present SAVEMem, a training-free dual-stage framework that brings semantic awareness into memory generation and lets the retrieval scope adapt per query. In Stage~1, SAVEMem builds a three-tier streaming memory online under a constant memory budget. A fixed pseudo-question bank provides a lightweight semantic prior, so that long-term retention is shaped by semantic salience rather than visual similarity alone. In Stage~2, SAVEMem performs query-aware retrieval over this memory. An anchor-conditioned recency gate adapts the retrieval scope from short-term to mid- and long-term memory based on whether the query targets the present or the distant past. Within this scope, late interaction between query and memory tokens selects candidate frames for answering. Applied to Qwen2.5-VL without training, SAVEMem improves the OVO-Bench overall score from 52.27 to 62.69 and yields consistent gains on StreamingBench and ODV-Bench, while reducing peak GPU memory by 48\% at 128 frames over the backbone.

</details>


### [100] [One World, Dual Timeline: Decoupled Spatio-Temporal Gaussian Scene Graph for 4D Cooperative Driving Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07910)
*Yulong Chen,Xiaoyun Dong,Haoyu Zhang,Zongxian Yang,Lewei Xie,Xinke Li,Yifan Zhang,Kai Wang,Jianping Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对车路协同自动驾驶（VICAD）中多源相机时间异步导致动态场景重建出现鬼影的问题，提出DUST（解耦时空）高斯场景图。该方法为每个动态智能体维护一组共享高斯用于外观一致性，同时为每个数据源维护独立解耦的位姿轨迹以对齐真实时间戳，理论上证明了该方案可消除跨源梯度干扰。在V2X-Seq的26个序列上，动态区域PSNR提升3.2dB，Fréchet视频距离降低37.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯场景图方法隐式假设多源相机观测同步，为每个智能体每帧分配单一姿态，这在车路协同场景中不成立，导致梯度冲突和动态物体鬼影。作者证明任何单时间线公式都会产生与智能体速度和跨源时间偏移成二次方增长的不可约光度损失。

Method: 提出DUST高斯场景图：1）每个动态智能体维护共享规范高斯集保证外观一致性；2）为每个数据源维护解耦的位姿轨迹对齐真实捕获时间戳，使姿态梯度核呈块对角化消除跨源干扰；3）静态锚点引导的位姿校正管道纠正车-路标注空间错位；4）位姿正则化联合优化方案防止训练初期轨迹抖动和漂移。

Result: 在V2X-Seq的26个序列上，DUST达到最优性能：动态区域PSNR提升3.2dB，Fréchet视频距离降低37.7%，且在更大时间异步下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DUST通过解耦位姿轨迹从根本上解决了协同感知中时间异步导致的动态场景重建问题，理论证明了单时间线方法的不可约损失上界，并验证了方法在实际车路协同数据上的有效性。

Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic scenes from Vehicle-to-Infrastructure Cooperative Autonomous Driving (VICAD) data is fundamentally complicated by temporal asynchrony: vehicle and infrastructure cameras operate on independent clocks, capturing the same dynamic agent such as cars and pedestrians at different physical times. Existing Gaussian Scene Graph methods implicitly assume synchronized observations and assign a single pose per agent per frame, which is an assumption that breaks in cooperative settings, where the resulting gradient conflicts cause severe ghosting on dynamic agents. We identify this as a representation-level failure, not an optimization artifact: we prove that any single-timeline formulation incurs an irreducible photometric loss scaling quadratically with agent velocity and cross-source time offset. To resolve this, we propose Dust (DecoUpled Spatio-Temporal) Gaussian Scene Graph for 4D Cooperative Driving Reconstruction. DUST Gaussian Scene Graph shares a canonical Gaussian set per agent for appearance consistency, while maintaining decouple pose trajectories aligned to each source's true capture timestamps. We prove that this decoupling enables the pose-gradient kernel block-diagonal, eliminating cross-source interference entirely. To make Dust practical, we further introduce a static anchor-based pose correction pipeline that corrects spatio misalignment between vehicle and infrastructure annotations, and a pose-regularized joint optimization scheme that prevents trajectory jitter and drift during early training. On 26 sequences from V2X-Seq, DUST achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving dynamic-area PSNR by 3.2 dB over the strongest baseline and reducing Fréchet Video Distance by 37.7%, with keeping robustness under larger temporal asynchrony. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DUST-6A55.

</details>


### [101] [What Matters for Diffusion-Friendly Latent Manifold? Prior-Aligned Autoencoders for Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07915)
*Zhengrong Yue,Taihang Hu,Mengting Chen,Haiyu Zhang,Zihao Pan,Tao Liu,Zikang Wang,Jinsong Lan,Xiaoyong Zhu,Bo Zheng,Yali Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究潜空间流形的组织方式对扩散模型生成质量的影响，发现空间结构连贯性、局部流形连续性和全局流形语义是关键属性，并提出Prior-Aligned AutoEncoder (PAE)来显式塑造潜空间流形，在ImageNet 256x256上达到1.03的gFID，且训练收敛速度提升最多13倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分词器主要关注重建保真度或继承预训练表示，但什么样的潜空间对生成建模真正友好尚不明确。本文从潜流形组织的角度研究这一问题。

Method: 通过构建受控分词器变体，识别出扩散友好潜流形的三个关键属性：空间结构连贯性、局部流形连续性和全局流形语义。提出PAE，利用VFMs精炼先验和基于扰动的正则化，将这三个属性转化为显式训练目标。

Result: 在ImageNet 256x256上，PAE在相同训练设置下比现有分词器提升训练效率和生成质量，收敛速度最高提升13倍，达到1.03的gFID，实现新SOTA。

Conclusion: 潜流形的组织方式对潜扩散模型的生成质量至关重要，显式塑造扩散友好的流形比依赖重建保真度或继承先验更有效。

Abstract: Tokenizers are a crucial component of latent diffusion models, as they define the latent space in which diffusion models operate. However, existing tokenizers are primarily designed to improve reconstruction fidelity or inherit pretrained representations, leaving unclear what kind of latent space is truly friendly for generative modeling. In this paper, we study this question from the perspective of latent manifold organization. By constructing controlled tokenizer variants, we identify three key properties of a diffusion-friendly latent manifold: coherent spatial structure, local manifold continuity, and global manifold semantics. We find that these properties are more consistent with downstream generation quality than reconstruction fidelity. Motivated by this finding, we propose the Prior-Aligned AutoEncoder (PAE), which explicitly shapes the latent manifold instead of leaving diffusion-friendly manifold to emerge indirectly from reconstruction or inheritance. Specifically, PAE leverages refined priors derived from VFMs and perturbation-based regularization to turn spatial structure, local continuity, and global semantics into explicit training objectives. On ImageNet 256x256, PAE improves both training efficiency and generation quality over existing tokenizers, reaching performance comparable to RAE with up to 13x faster convergence under the same training setup and achieving a new state-of-the-art gFID of 1.03. These results highlight the importance of organizing the latent manifold for latent diffusion models.

</details>


### [102] [MedVIGIL: Evaluating Trustworthy Medical VLMs Under Broken Visual Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07919)
*Hanqi Jiang,Junhao Chen,Yi Pan,Lifeng Chen,Weihang You,Haozhen Gong,Ruiyu Yan,Jinglei Lv,Lin Zhao,Hui Ren,Quanzheng Li,Tianming Liu,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出medvigil基准测试集，用于评估医学视觉语言模型在证据失效时的静默失败问题，包含300例由放射科医生监督构建的病例、2556个MCQ探针和多项审计指标。独立放射科医生MCS得分83.3，最强模型（Claude Opus 4.7）仅69.2，表明当前模型在可靠拒绝方面存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学VLM评估通常基于完整的图像-问题对，但临床可信使用要求模型能在证据基础失效时识别并拒绝回答。当前缺乏系统性的基准来评估模型面对扰动证据时的静默失败行为。

Method: 构建medvigil基准集：从4个公开医学VQA来源精选300例，由4位认证放射科医师端到端监督；每个病例包含金标准答案、拒绝选项、虚假前提陷阱、ROI框等；设置7种正确性条件审计指标并汇总为MCS综合评分；审计16个视觉语言模型及2个纯文本基线。

Result: 独立放射科医生MCS 83.3，静默失败率5.8%；最强模型Claude Opus 4.7得分69.2，存在14.1分的复合分差；所有模型在证据扰动场景下均存在显著的静默失败问题。

Conclusion: 当前最强医学VLM在证据失效场景下的可靠拒绝能力与人类专家存在显著差距，medvigil为可靠临床AI评估提供了标准化基准。

Abstract: Medical vision--language models (VLMs) are usually evaluated on intact image--question pairs, but trustworthy clinical use requires a stronger property: a model must recognise when the evidential basis for an answer has failed. We study this through silent failures under perturbed evidence, where a vision-required medical question is paired with a false premise, wording perturbation, knowledge-only rewrite, or ROI-corrupted image, yet the model returns a fluent non-refusal answer. We introduce medvigil, a 300-case evaluation suite drawn from four public medical VQA sources, supervised end to end by four board-certified radiologists: every gold answer, refusal option, candidate-answer set, paraphrase, false-premise trap, ROI box, and clinical risk tier is clinician-authored. Two attending radiologists annotate every case in parallel, a senior radiologist consolidates the released manifest, and a separate fourth radiologist independent of construction answers every probe to provide the human reference baseline. The release contains 2{,}556 MCQ probes, 240 counterfactual triplets, physician-adjudicated risk-tier and answerability flags, ROI boxes, and a paired open-ended variant. We report seven correctness-conditioned audit metrics that summarise into the medvigil Composite Score (MCS), and audit 16 vision-capable models plus two text-only baselines. The independent radiologist scores MCS 83.3 at silent-failure rate 5.8%, leaving a 14.1-point composite headroom above the strongest audited model (Claude Opus 4.7 at 69.2). The benchmark and evaluation harness are publicly released.

</details>


### [103] [One Token Per Frame: Reconsidering Visual Bandwidth in World Models for VLA Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07931)
*Zuojin Tang,Shengchao Yuan,Xiaoxin Bai,Zhiyuan Jin,De Ma,Gang Pan,Bin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OneWM-VLA通过将每帧视觉信息压缩为单个语义token，并在统一流匹配目标下联合建模潜在流和动作轨迹，在冻结VLA骨干上以少量LoRA参数显著提升长程任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型增强的VLA方法以高视觉带宽逐帧传递视觉流，且将世界模型 rollout 视为动作预测的副产品，在冻结骨干的有限适配预算下，逐帧表示和潜在动作耦合均未被充分探索。

Method: 1) 自适应注意力池化(Adaptive Attention Pooling)将每帧压缩为单个语义token；2) 在单一流匹配(flow-matching)目标下联合建模压缩后的潜在流和动作轨迹，而非通过独立解码器连接。

Result: 仅14.71M LoRA参数在π₀ (2B)骨干上：MetaWorld MT50平均成功率47.9%→61.3%；LIBERO-Long 85.2%→95.6%；真实Piper机械臂长程可变形折叠任务20.0%→60.0%。

Conclusion: 每帧视觉带宽可降至单个token而不损害长程任务性能；统一流匹配目标能有效解耦并联合优化潜在表示与动作预测。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models increasingly rely on auxiliary world modules to plan over long horizons, yet how such modules should be parameterized on top of a pretrained VLA remains an open design question. Existing world-model-augmented VLAs typically pass the per-frame visual stream into the world module at high visual bandwidth and treat its rollout as a side product of action prediction; under a constrained adaptation budget on a frozen backbone, this leaves both the per-frame representation and the latent action coupling under-examined. We introduce OneWM-VLA, which compresses each view into a single semantic token per frame through an Adaptive Attention Pooling, and produces the resulting latent stream and the action trajectory under a single flow-matching objective rather than connecting them through a separate decoder. Empirically, we find that per-frame visual bandwidth can be reduced to a single token without compromising long-horizon performance under our setup. Trained with 14.71M LoRA parameters on a $π_0$ (2B) backbone, OneWM-VLA improves the average success rate from 47.9% to 61.3% on MetaWorld~MT50, reaches 95.6% on LIBERO-Long (vs.85.2% for $π_0$), and reaches 60.0% on the long-horizon deformable task Fold Cloth on a real Piper arm (vs.20.0% for $π_0$).

</details>


### [104] [Delta-Adapter: Scalable Exemplar-Based Image Editing with Single-Pair Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07940)
*Jiacheng Chen,Songze Li,Han Fu,Baoquan Zhao,Wei Liu,Yanyan Liang,Li Qing,Xudong Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Delta-Adapter提出了一种基于单对样本监督的范例图像编辑方法，通过预训练视觉编码器提取语义增量（semantic delta）并借助Perceiver适配器注入预训练编辑模型，无需文本引导即可学习可迁移的编辑语义，在减少数据需求的同时提升了编辑准确性和内容一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有范例图像编辑方法依赖双对样本（两对共享相同编辑语义的源-目标图像对）的监督范式，导致训练数据难以大规模构建，且限制了模型对多样化编辑类型的泛化能力。

Method: 利用预训练视觉编码器从范例对中提取语义增量（semantic delta），即编码两幅图像之间视觉变换的语义表示；通过Perceiver-based适配器将该语义增量注入预训练图像编辑模型；目标图像不直接暴露给模型，而是作为预测目标，从而支持单对样本监督；引入语义增量一致性损失函数，确保生成输出的语义变化与范例对中提取的真实语义增量对齐。

Result: 在四个强基线方法上的实验表明，Delta-Adapter在已见编辑任务上持续提升了编辑准确性和内容一致性，同时能够更有效地泛化到未见编辑任务。

Conclusion: Delta-Adapter通过单对样本监督和语义增量机制，有效降低了数据依赖，提升了编辑迁移的保真度和泛化能力，为范例图像编辑提供了一种更高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Exemplar-based image editing applies a transformation defined by a source-target image pair to a new query image. Existing methods rely on a pair-of-pairs supervision paradigm, requiring two image pairs sharing the same edit semantics to learn the target transformation. This constraint makes training data difficult to curate at scale and limits generalization across diverse edit types. We propose Delta-Adapter, a method that learns transferable editing semantics under single-pair supervision, requiring no textual guidance. Rather than directly exposing the exemplar pair to the model, we leverage a pre-trained vision encoder to extract a semantic delta that encodes the visual transformation between the two images. This semantic delta is injected into a pre-trained image editing model via a Perceiver-based adapter. Since the target image is never directly visible to the model, it can serve as the prediction target, enabling single-pair supervision without requiring additional exemplar pairs. This formulation allows us to leverage existing large-scale editing datasets for training. To further promote faithful transformation transfer, we introduce a semantic delta consistency loss that aligns the semantic change of the generated output with the ground-truth semantic delta extracted from the exemplar pair. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Delta-Adapter consistently improves both editing accuracy and content consistency over four strong baselines on seen editing tasks, while also generalizing more effectively to unseen editing tasks. Code will be available at https://delta-adapter.github.io.

</details>


### [105] [DVD: Discrete Voxel Diffusion for 3D Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07971)
*Zhengrui Xiang,Jiaqi Wu,Fupeng Sun,Heliang Zheng,Yingzhen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出离散体素扩散（DVD）框架，将离散扩散模型应用于SLat（结构化潜在）3D生成流水线中的稀疏体素生成、评估与编辑任务。通过将体素占据概率视为原生离散变量，DVD避免了连续到离散的阈值处理，并提供了体素生成、不确定性估计和编辑的简洁框架。利用预测熵作为鲁棒的不确定性度量，可识别模糊体素区域和复杂样本。此外，提出一种轻量级微调策略，使用块结构扰动模式，使模型在单轮采样中实现体素修复和编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散在图像生成中尚未取代连续扩散，但作者证明其在稀疏体素支架上可作为有效的先验模型。现有方法需要连续到离散的阈值处理，缺乏解释性，且难以进行不确定性估计和灵活编辑。

Method: 1) 将体素占据概率作为原生离散变量进行扩散建模；2) 使用预测熵作为不确定性度量，识别模糊区域和复杂样本；3) 提出块结构扰动模式的轻量级微调策略，实现单轮采样下的体素修复与编辑。

Result: DVD在体素生成质量上超越基线；预测熵能够有效识别模糊体素区域和复杂样本，支持数据过滤和质量评估；所提微调策略能在不增加额外模型评估的情况下高效完成体素修复和编辑。

Conclusion: 离散扩散在稀疏体素生成中是一种有效且更具解释性的第一阶先验方法，统一了生成、评估和编辑任务，为SLat类3D生成流水线提供了更简洁的解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce Discrete Voxel Diffusion (DVD), a discrete diffusion framework to generate, assess, and edit sparse voxels for SLat (Structured LATent) based 3D generative pipelines. Although discrete diffusion has not generally displaced continuous diffusion in image-like generation, we show that it can be an effective first-stage prior for sparse voxel scaffolds. By treating voxel occupancy as a native discrete variable, DVD avoids continuous-to-discrete thresholding and provides a simple framework for voxel generation, uncertainty estimation, and editing. Beyond quality gains, DVD provides more interpretable generation dynamics through explicit categorical modeling. Furthermore, we leverage the predictive entropy as a robust uncertainty metric to identify ambiguous voxel regions and complicated samples, facilitating tasks such as data filtering and quality assessment. Finally, we propose a lightweight fine-tuning strategy using block-structured perturbation patterns. This approach empowers the model to inpaint and edit voxels within a single sampling round, requiring negligible auxiliary computation and no additional model evaluations.

</details>


### [106] [HEART: Hyperspherical Embedding Alignment via Kent-Representation Traversal in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07973)
*Arani Roy,Shristi Das Biswas,Kaushik Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现文本到图像扩散模型的文本嵌入实际位于超球面上，而非欧几里得空间，并提出无需训练的HEART框架，通过在超球面上进行Kent分布感知的测地变换，实现精确可控的图像编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于文本的图像控制方法将嵌入空间视为欧几里得空间并应用线性变换，这不符合语义概念的实际组织方式，导致控制时出现背景改变、细节失真等副作用，因此需要探究文本嵌入的真实几何结构。

Method: 首先发现文本编码器嵌入位于超球面上，概念表现为各向异性的Kent分布而非线性方向；进而提出HEART框架，直接在超球面上进行Kent感知的测地变换，无需微调、反演或优化。

Result: HEART实现了直观精确的图像编辑，包括一致的主体替换和细粒度属性控制，同时保持原始场景不变，且能泛化到不同扩散模型架构。

Conclusion: 将视角从线性空间转变为球面几何，可以解锁快速可控的图像生成，证明尊重数据的底层几何结构是提升控制精度的关键。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models can generate visually stunning images, yet, controlling what appears and how it appears, remains surprisingly difficult, especially when operating solely within the constraints of the text-conditioning space. For example, changing a subject or adjusting an attribute often leads to unintended side effects, such as altered backgrounds or distorted details. This is because most existing text-based control methods treat the embedding space as Euclidean and apply simple linear transformations, which do not reflect how semantic concepts are actually organized. In this work, we take a step back and ask: what is the true geometry of these embeddings? We find that text encoder representations lie on a hypersphere, where concepts are not linear directions but structured, anisotropic distributions better captured by Kent distributions. Building on this insight, we propose HEART, a training-free framework that performs Kent-aware geodesic transformations directly on the hypersphere. By respecting the underlying geometry, HEART enables intuitive and precise edits, such as consistent subject replacement and fine-grained attribute control, while preserving the original scene. Importantly, HEART requires no finetuning, inversion, or optimization, and generalizes across diffusion model architectures. Our results show that a simple shift in perspective, from linear to spherical, can unlock fast, and controllable image generation.

</details>


### [107] [Seeing Across Skies and Streets: Feedforward 3D Reconstruction from Satellite, Drone, and Ground Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07978)
*Qiwei Wang,Zhongyao Tuo,Xianghui Ze,Yujiao Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Cross3R模型，通过引入无人机图像作为中间视角，实现从卫星图到地面图的6自由度定位与3D点云重建，同时构建了覆盖全球85个场景的278K图像三视图数据集CrossGeo。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视角定位方法仅能估计3自由度(x,y,yaw)，依赖平面运动和零倾斜假设，在真实地形(斜坡、相机倾斜安装)下失效。需引入额外视角提供3D结构信息，实现完整6自由度估计。
